- **ceq-fit-positioning**: Use to judge whether a manuscript is on-target for 《经济学(季刊)》 (China Economic Quarterly, CEQ) before investing in revision — CEQ is the strictest Chinese econ journal on identification and the most internationally legible. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/China-Economic-Quarterly-Skills/skills/ceq-fit-positioning) · ★1.1k)
- **ceq-identification**: Use when the identification strategy is the bottleneck for a 《经济学(季刊)》 (China Economic Quarterly, CEQ) manuscript — either a structural model with credible parameters, or a clean quasi-experiment (DID / IV / RDD / event-study). CEQ is the strictest Chinese econ journal here; identification assumptions must be made explicit and each defended. Stress-test the design before drafting exhibits. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/China-Economic-Quarterly-Skills/skills/ceq-identification) · ★1.1k)

([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/China-Economic-Quarterly-Skills/skills/ceq-inference) · ★1.1k)
- **ceq-literature-review**: Use when positioning a 《经济学(季刊)》 (China Economic Quarterly, CEQ) manuscript's contribution against the literature — benchmarking the precise method/result delta versus specific field papers (English + Chinese), not piling citations. Use after the topic is set and before/alongside identification. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/China-Economic-Quarterly-Skills/skills/ceq-literature-review) · ★1.1k)
- **ceq-mechanism**: Use when a 《经济学(季刊)》 (China Economic Quarterly, CEQ) manuscript has a credible main effect but the channel/mechanism is asserted rather than evidenced — to design tests that distinguish competing channels and provide falsifiable mechanism evidence. Use after identification is settled. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/China-Economic-Quarterly-Skills/skills/ceq-mechanism) · ★1.1k)
- **ceq-modern-did**: Use when a 《经济学(季刊)》 (China Economic Quarterly, CEQ) manuscript uses difference-in-differences with staggered adoption or heterogeneous treatment effects — to bring it into compliance with modern estimators (Callaway–Sant'Anna, de Chaisemartin–D'Haultfœuille, Sun–Abraham) instead of bare two-way fixed effects. - **ceq-rebuttal**: Use when drafting the response letter for a revise-and-resubmit (R&R) at 《经济学(季刊)》 (China Economic Quarterly, CEQ) — where reviewers are typically field-trained and press hardest on identification, modern-DID compliance, and inference.
